# Victim Reaction Animations - what already exists

Catalogue of the animations a fighter plays when something is done **to** them:
throws, fatality holds, decapitation falls, the Finish Him dizzy, the soul-suck
drain pose. Written to answer "what can I reuse?" before authoring new art.

Generated by `python tools/survey_victim_anis.py --anipoints`. Re-run it after
any anitab or reaction-table edit; everything below the divider is its output.

---

## How a fatality poses its victim

There are exactly **two** mechanisms, and most fatalities use both.

### 1. Slot lookup - `get_his_char_ani`

Every fighter has an `anitab1` (indexed by `ochar` through
`character_anitabs1` in [MKGUYS.ASM](../src/MKGUYS.ASM)). The slot layout is
fixed across all fighters, so an attacker can say "put him in *his own*
version of reaction N":

```asm
	movi	038h,a9			; slot 0x38 = drained
	calla	get_his_char_ani	; a9 = HIS a_drained
	move	a9,a11
	movk	1,a0
	jsrp	mframew_him
```

The attacker never needs to know who the victim is. This is why every fighter
has a `*DRAIN0`, a `*DECAP1-7`, a `*STUNNED1-7` and so on - the slot has to be
filled for all of them or the lookup reads garbage.

Live slot users (from a scan of the whole tree):

| slot | reaction | reached from |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0x0F` | knocked down | `goro_pycho_hit` (MKBOSS) |
| `0x10` | hit high | `kahn_bigp_pose_hithi` (MKBOSS) |
| `0x11` | hit low | `bike8` (MKPROP) |
| `0x16` | sweep fall | `scissor_hit` (MKPROP) |
| `0x28` | stunned / Finish Him dizzy | `dizzy_wait` (MKDEATH) |
| `0x2A`..`0x35` | thrown, one slot per thrower `ochar` | `flip4` (MKSLAM) |
| `0x38` | **drained by Shang Tsung** | `do_drain` (MKDEATH) |
| `0x39` | torso ripped | `do_rip`, `drag4` (MKDEATH) |
| `0x3B` | impaled | `do_impale` (MKDEATH) |
| `0x3D` | decapitated head | `do_tongue` (MKDEATH) |
| `0x3E` | flipped by Kintaro | `goro_slam` (MKBOSS) |
| `0x3F` | back broken | `do_back_breaker` (MKSLAM) |

### 2. Process handoff - `takeover_him` / `takeover_him_sr`

For anything with its own physics or timing, the attacker hands the victim's
process to a routine instead:

```asm
	movi	decap_fall,a7
	calla	takeover_him_sr		; _sr = also drop repell
```

`takeover_him` stops him, disables his buttons, clears his action, sets
`pm_reacting`, deletes his slave objects - **and calls `player_normpal`**.
That last one matters: it will undo a palette swap you just applied. Fatalities
that recolour the victim (the `CORPSE_P` husk, bloody palettes) must swap
*after* the handoff, not before.

Existing victim routines, and who hands off to them:

| --- | --- |
| `decap_fall` | `do_decap`, `do_fan_decap`, `do_st_decap`, `stfdec3` |
| `flipped_pause` | `do_back_breaker`, `goro_slam`, `goro_pycho_hit`, `hathead_slam`, `bsi3` |
| `r_second_swipe` | `do_spear_swipe`, `do_raiden_bolt_decap`, `dikil3`, `rbd_no_bolt` |
| `r_burst_pop` | `do_st_dive`, `kahn_portal_try_fatal` |

## Worked example - the soul suck (`do_drain`)

What you see as "Shang grabs them" is three separate things stacked:

1. `takeover_him` -> `wait_forever` freezes the victim where he stands.
2. `get_his_char_ani 0x38` -> **his own `a_drained`**, which is one stumble
   frame then his `*DRAIN0` soul-suck pose held x4, every entry preceded by
   `ani_adjustxy` - that is the wobble.
3. `drain_table` (MKFX.ASM) supplies the drained palette, and
   `alternate_suck_pals` a right-side variant. Both are `ochar`-indexed.
4. `a_final_drain` -> `CORPSE1A`, the desiccated husk, then (new)
   `a_corpse_drop` -> `CORPSEDROP1-8` for the collapse.

So the "grab" is not a grab animation at all - there is no contact art. The
victim simply stops, wobbles in place in his drain pose, and recolours.

## Reading the tables below

- **beats** - a table is split by its `0` terminators into beats. Fatality
  controllers walk them one `mframew` at a time, so a beat boundary is a place
  the attacker can do something (fire a sound, swap a palette) between frames.
- **`xN`** - the same frame repeated N times, i.e. an authored hold.
- **`*(wobble: ani_adjustxy)*`** - the beat contains `ani_adjustxy`, which
  nudges the object by a `.word dx,dy` before the frame. This is how every
  stumble/drain shake is done: `ani0` normally moves an object by the anipoint
  delta between consecutive frames, and `adjustxy` is the manual override on
  top of that.
- **`*(ani_jump)*`** - the table loops forever (all the `0x28` dizzy loops).
- **entry -> pieces** - ani entries are multipart **wrappers**, not sprite
  records. `TMSTUNNED1` is `.long TMSTUNNED1A,TMSTUNNED1B,0`; only the pieces
  exist in a `.TBL`. Player objects are multipart, so any new entry you author
  must be a piece list too - a bare image header scrambles the display walker.
- **floor (h-aniy)** - distance from the anipoint to the bottom of the sprite.
  Frames in one lane that share this value sit on a common floor line; a lane
  where it drifts is one where the body rises or sinks.

## Caveats worth knowing before you reuse a slot

- **Shared tables.** Kitana, Mileena and Jade share one `anitab1`
  (`fn1_/fn2_/fn3_anitab1` are stacked labels on the same body). Sub-Zero,
  Reptile, Scorpion, Smoke and Noob share another (`sz_/rp_/sc_/sm_anitab1`).
  Editing a reaction for one edits it for all of them. Shang Tsung's
  `a_decapfall` even borrows the *ninja* `NJDECAP` art outright.
- **The bosses have no victim slots at all.** `go_anitab1` (Kintaro) stops at
  slot `0x17` and `sk_anitab1` (Shao Kahn) at `0x19`, against `0x42` for a
  normal fighter. They are not fatality victims and cannot be made into ones
  without extending both tables. Every "(no slot)" row below is one of them.
- **Slot lookups are unbounded.** `get_his_char_ani` does not range-check, so
  a short table reads past its end - the same class as the `ochar` dispatch
  tables audited by `tools/audit_char_tables.py`.
- **Frame names are not always spelled the way you expect.** Baraka's stumble
  is `UGSTUMBL1E1`, not `UGSTUMBLE1`, and its own wrapper mixes the two
  (`.long UGSTUMBL1E1A,UGSTUMBLE1B,0`). That is Midway's typo, preserved in the
  leak and in the `.TBL`. Grep the table below rather than guessing a name.
